The learning phase was followed by exploration of an unfamiliar dataset using alt text and an LLM. Thematic analysis shows that tactile templates support BLV participants' formation of chart-type mental models, which scaffolds subsequent LLM-mediated data exploration. Text+LLM explanations without tactile support show weaknesses for spatial-reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [177] [From Vibe to Code -- and Back: Lexical Oscillation in the Formation of Design Intent with Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23126)
*Daisaku Sato*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过分析5位资深UI/UX设计师与生成式AI工具的交互过程，发现设计意图并非预先存在，而是在与AI的互动中形成。设计师的提示语言呈现词法振荡（在模糊的"氛围"与具体的"操作语言"之间来回切换），而非单向从模糊走向具体。AI输出的不匹配反而成为设计师重新思考自身意图的契机，交互模式从"指令"转向"咨询"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点将提示词视为设计师传递预先存在的设计意图的工具，但本研究质疑这一假设，试图理解设计意图如何在人与生成式AI的互动情境中形成。

Method: 5位资深UI/UX设计师（经验11-20年，平均15.4年）使用生成式AI工具设计着陆页首屏。采用出声思维法和回顾性访谈，运用反身性主题分析，以词法粒度（L1氛围语言、L2设计领域语言、L3操作语言）作为敏化视角进行分析。

Result: 设计师的提示词并非单向从氛围（L1）走向代码（L3），而是呈现词法振荡，包括从操作具体性回到模糊性。AI输出的不匹配被设计师用作重新思考自身意图的契机，交互模式从"指令"转向"咨询"。发现一个非振荡的负面案例，提示概念错位可能作为未来研究的边界条件。

Conclusion: 将AI定位为非中立的生成性对话者，将模糊性视为设计判断的资源。

Abstract: Generative AI design tools make natural-language prompts a starting point for design, placing new articulation demands on designers. Rather than treating prompts as the transmission of pre-existing design intent, we ask how design intent is formed through situated interaction with AI. Five expert UI/UX designers (11-20 years' experience, M = 15.4) designed landing-page hero sections with a generative AI tool, recorded through think-aloud and retrospective interviews. Using reflexive thematic analysis, we used lexical granularity (L1 vibe, L2 design-domain, L3 operational language) as a sensitizing lens. Rather than moving from vibe to code unidirectionally, designers showed lexical oscillation, including returns from operational specificity to ambiguity. Mismatches with AI outputs were taken up as occasions for designers to reconsider what they meant, and engagement shifted from instruction to consultation. One non-oscillating trajectory -- a negative case -- suggested conceptual misalignment as a tentative boundary for future examination. We position AI as a non-neutral generative interlocutor and ambiguity as a resource for design judgment.

</details>


### [178] [Beyond Conversations: Spatially-Anchored Previews for Intent Disambiguation in LLM-Assisted Geometry Editing in Virtual Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23201)
*Junlong Chen,Amr Gomaa,Jens Grubert,Per Ola Kristensson*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在VR/AR环境中，如何将传统的基于对话的意图消歧与空间锚定的图形预览相结合，以解决LLM辅助编辑工作流中的模糊用户指令问题。通过24名受试者的实验，发现混合方法（澄清问题+图形预览）相比无消歧方案，能减少对话轮次、提升交互稳定性和用户体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户意图消歧在智能交互系统中仍是关键挑战，尤其是在沉浸式环境（VR/AR）下的LLM辅助编辑工作流中，相关研究尚不充分。传统2D界面中的对话消歧方法无法直接迁移，需要探索新的消歧方式。

Method: 采用受试者内设计（within-subjects study），24名参与者在VR中完成复杂几何编辑任务，场景由数值参数驱动。对比条件为：无消歧 vs. 混合消歧（澄清问题+图形预览）。通过定量指标（对话轮次、交互稳定性）和定性反馈评估效果。

Result: 混合消歧方法相比无消歧条件，能显著减少对话轮次，提升交互稳定性，并改善用户体验。

Conclusion: 研究为LLM辅助的沉浸式场景参数驱动编辑中的消歧方法有效性提供了实证证据，并为未来VR/AR系统中集成LLM的设计提供了指导原则。

Abstract: User intent disambiguation remains a key challenge in intelligent interactive systems. While they have been widely studied in dialogue systems in 2D interfaces, research on how intent disambiguation could be incorporated within Large Language Model (LLM) assisted editing workflows in immersive environments remains limited. Recent advances in LLMs create opportunities to leverage the immersive nature of virtual and augmented reality (VR/AR) environments to provide better disambiguation support. In this paper, we evaluate how traditional dialogue-based disambiguation can be augmented with spatially-anchored graphical previews to resolve ambiguous user commands in LLM-assisted parameter-driven editing workflows. A within-subjects study in which 24 participants completed complex geometry editing tasks in VR simulate scenarios where VR scenes are controlled by numerical parameters. Compared with the condition where disambiguation is not available, quantitative metrics and qualitative feedback indicate that a hybrid approach which combines clarification questions and graphical previews can support better interaction stability with fewer conversation rounds while improving user experience. These findings provide empirical evidence on the effectiveness of disambiguation methods in LLM-assisted editing of parameter-driven immersive scenes and inform design guidelines for future integration of LLMs in advanced VR/AR systems.

</details>


### [179] [A Taxonomy of Confabulations and the Perception-Reality Gap in LLM-Assisted Immersive Scene Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23213)
*Junlong Chen,Per Ola Kristensson*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过24名非专业用户的探索性研究，构建了LLM在沉浸式3D场景编辑中产生“虚构”（confabulation）错误的分类体系，分析了其普遍性和干扰性，定义了“感知-现实差距”概念，并探讨了减轻此类错误的设计启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在沉浸式3D场景编辑中常出现错误（虚构），但目前缺乏对这一错误类型的系统研究，尤其是从非专业用户视角理解其影响。

Method: 开展一项包含24名非专业用户的探索性研究，观察他们在LLM辅助的沉浸式3D场景编辑过程中的表现，构建错误分类体系并测量其感知差异。

Result: 构建了LLM在沉浸式3D场景编辑中虚构错误的分类体系；报告了错误的普遍性和干扰程度；定义了“感知-现实差距”概念；发现用户在高负荷下对错误察觉能力趋于饱和。

Conclusion: 提出了未来LLM辅助沉浸式3D系统中减轻虚构错误的设计启示，强调了在高认知负荷场景下增强用户对错误意识的重要性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are being increasingly integrated into immersive environments and design workflows, providing application prospects in areas such as rapid scene prototyping for non-expert users and scene understanding capabilities for accessibility design. While many workflows that incorporate LLMs in immersive spaces are proposed, such systems can exhibit errors, potentially resulting in frustration, loss of user trust, and compromised user safety. This paper studies the underexplored area of LLM confabulations in immersive 3D scene editing contexts. Through an exploratory study with 24 non-expert users, we construct a taxonomy of the different types of confabulation observed in LLM-assisted immersive 3D scene editing. We report their prevalence and disruptiveness, and define the construct perception-reality gap to help understand the gap between the actual and perceived occurrence of confabulations. We highlight the observed saturation of confabulation awareness under load and conclude by discussing design implications for confabulation mitigation in future LLM-assisted systems in immersive 3D scenes.

</details>


### [180] [Cross Sensory Co-Design Tools and Interaction Qualities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23298)
*Albrecht Kurze,Klaus Stephan*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了两个用于跨感官和多模态情感交互的协同设计工具（Loaded Dice 和 Wheel of Plush），它们通过技术联觉实现传感器与执行器的任意映射，支持探索、感官草图绘制和场景驱动的创意生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨感官和多模态交互在情感设计中具有巨大潜力，但缺乏合适的协同设计工具来支持此类交互的创建。

Method: 开发了 Loaded Dice 和 Wheel of Plush 两个工具，采用技术联觉原理，实现传感器到执行器的任意映射，并提供直观简单的交互方案。

Result: 两个工具成功实现了传感器与执行器之间的有意义映射，支持探索性使用、感官草图绘制和场景驱动的创意生成。

Conclusion: 这些工具为跨感官情感交互设计提供了有效的协同设计手段，并具有进一步的应用潜力。

Abstract: What is the temperature of a loud cry? What is the color of a hand-clap? Cross-sensory and multi-modal interactions offer interesting possibilities in terms of inputs and outputs that might be leveraged for affective and emotional purposes. However, it is hard to design such interactions without the right co-design tools. We developed such tools in the last 10 years: the Loaded Dice and the Wheel of Plush. Both devices incorporate a multitude of sensors and actuators to demonstrate interactions and aid in co-design workshops. The tools incorporate the principle of technical synesthesia: the ability to map any of the included sensors to any of the included actuators. With intuitive simple interaction schemes it is possible to easily and effortlessly create new combinations. We discuss the core principles of the tools and how we realized a meaningful mapping between sensors and actuators. We further discuss how we use the tools for exploration, sensory sketching or scenario driven ideation, and where we see additional potential.

</details>


### [181] [Explainable AI through the Lens of Material Agency: Enabling Musical Interface Design with Neural Audio Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23309)
*Shuoyang Jasper Zheng,Anna Xambó Sedó,Nick Bryan-Kinns*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出"物质可解释性"概念，旨在将AI模型转化为艺术家、设计师和创作者的易用设计材料，通过构建神经音频模型资源库的案例研究，总结了促进AI作为艺术设计材料探索的三条建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HCI研究将AI视为设计材料，但艺术家缺乏理解和操作AI模型的工具与空间，可解释性需要服务于艺术家的创造性实践而非仅用于技术理解。

Method: 采用案例研究方法，构建了一个面向NIME（新乐器表达接口）设计的神经音频模型资源库，与艺术家群体共同创作音乐接口设计，并进行社区建设反思。

Result: 通过社区建设实践，提出了三条促进AI作为艺术设计材料探索的建议，为未来面向艺术家的XAI设计提供指导。

Conclusion: 物质可解释性能够将AI模型转变为艺术家可访问、可包容的设计材料，从而支撑持续性的AI创意实践。

Abstract: Recent work in Human-Computer Interaction (HCI) increasingly treats AI models as design materials that have distinctive computational properties to shape design artifacts. Artists learn to work with the model "at play" to explore their emerging properties. The aim of explainability, in this view, is to make visible a crafting and hacking space to enable sustained creative practices with AI. In this chapter, we propose material explainability as a range of activities and artifacts that transform AI models into accessible and inclusive design materials in the workspace of artists, designers, and makers. We present a case study of building a repository of resources to enable artistic explorations of neural audio models in New Interfaces for Musical Expression (NIME) design. Reflecting on our community-building journey and the making of a collection of musical interface designs with a group of artists, we raise three recommendations on enabling the exploration of AI as materials in artistic practices to inspire future XAI design for artists.

</details>


### [182] [Visualizing in the Mind's Eye: Icon Design Shapes Mental Imagery of Fire Risks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23369)
*Wen Xu,Anjana Arunkumar,Lace M. Padilla*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究发现，抽象几何图标比具象火灾图标更能激发心理意象，进而通过负面情绪引发风险规避决策。具体设计可能通过限制心理意象来抑制情感驱动的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索数据可视化设计如何通过激发心理意象（"脑海中的图像"）这一认知过程来影响人们的决策。

Method: 预注册实验（n=400），对比抽象几何图标与具象房屋着火图标在呈现火灾风险数据时的效果，并通过中介分析检验心理意象、负面情绪与决策之间的关系。

Result: 抽象几何图标比具象图标更能激发心理意象，且产生的意象更多样化和个性化。心理意象通过唤起负面情绪，进而促使风险规避决策。

Conclusion: 可视化具体性影响决策的机制比预期更复杂：具体设计可能通过限制心理意象来抑制情感驱动的行为，而抽象设计则通过激发心理意象增强情感反应。

Abstract: We introduce mental imagery, or seeing images in the "mind's eye," as a cognitive process that can be shaped by data visualization design and in turn impact decisions. We found in a preregistered study (n = 400) that abstract geometric icons visualizing fire risk data evoked more mental images than concrete house-on-fire icons, and also produced more diverse and personalized mental images. Mediation analysis showed that increased mental imagery subsequently led to risk-averse decisions through evoking negative affect. These findings reveal a nuanced mechanism through which visualization concreteness influences decisions: concrete designs may actually suppress affect-driven behavior by restricting mental imagery.

</details>


### [183] [Multimodal Data Comprehension: Understanding How Visual-Textual Chains of Information Influence Data Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23489)
*Arran Zeyu Wang,Fuling Sun,Danielle Albers Szafir*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过实验研究多模态（可视化+文本）数据理解过程，发现可视化优先链促进探索性理解，文本优先链产生确认性理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管可视化和文本常共同呈现数据，但人们对这两种模态如何相互作用影响数据理解知之甚少。

Method: 采用两种顺序链条（可视化优先和文本优先）呈现多模态信息，通过众包和有声思维实验，比较人们数据理解的变化。

Result: 可视化优先链促进探索性、假设驱动的理解；文本优先链产生确认性理解，类似框架效应，可视化用于强化文本中的观察。

Conclusion: 为多模态信息整合提供了实证见解，对设计更有效的数据驱动传播具有启示意义。

Abstract: Visualizations and text often work together to support effective data communication. Despite this common paradigm, we know little about how the interplay of these modalities affects people's data comprehension. We present a novel experimental paradigm to investigate multimodal data comprehension---the process of people comprehending information from multimodal visual and textual data---across both crowdsourced and think-aloud environments. Our methodology employs two sequential chains for presenting multimodal information---a visualization-first chain and a text-first chain---asking people to describe the data presented iteratively. By comparing how people's data comprehension changes across the chain, we can assess the information contribution of each modality and how they shape subsequent comprehension. We found that the visualization-first chain facilitates exploratory comprehension with hypothesis-driven discovery, whereas the text-first chain yields confirmatory comprehension akin to framing effects where visualizations serve to reinforce and confirm observations drawn from text. Our findings provide empirical insights into multimodal information integration, with implications for designing more effective data-driven communication.

</details>


### [184] [Plans Work in Mysterious Ways: Evaluating a Plan Mode for Spreadsheet Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23670)
*Aayush Kumar,Avik Dutta,Sumit Gulwani,Gustavo Soares,Advait Sarkar,Emerson Murphy-Hill*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在电子表格编程中引入计划模式（Plan Mode）的效果。通过用户实验（N=24）发现，尽管任务结果相似，但计划模式能减少迭代修改次数，并提升用户对工具在创造力支持和人机协作方面的感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计划模式已在智能体编程工具中成为标准功能，但其在电子表格这类终端用户编程环境中的适用性尚不明确。由于电子表格程序员倾向于迭代式工作且不特别关注技术正确性，前置计划可能难以融入他们的工作流程。

Method: 构建了一个电子表格编程的计划模式原型，并通过组内用户实验（N=24）将其与无计划模式的基线工具进行对比评估。

Result: 使用计划模式与不使用在任务结果上无显著差异，但计划模式减少了迭代修改次数，并在创造力支持和人机协作维度上获得了更好的用户感知评分。

Conclusion: 讨论了计划模式在电子表格编程中的设计启示，以及人机规划在终端用户编程中的更广泛意义。

Abstract: Plan Modes have become standard features in agentic programming tools, allowing users to gain transparency and control by working with the agent to develop a plan before task execution. However, it remains unclear whether the benefits of this feature translate to end-user programming environments such as spreadsheets. Since spreadsheet programmers tend to work iteratively and care less about technical correctness, upfront planning may not fit into their workflows as easily. In this paper, we build a prototype of a Plan Mode for spreadsheet programming and evaluate it against a non-planning baseline through a within-subjects user study (N=24). We found that despite similar task outcomes with both tools, using Plan Mode led to a reduction in refinement and a better perception of the tool across dimensions of creativity support and human-machine collaboration. We discuss the implications of these results for the future design of Plan Modes, and for the broader role of human-AI planning in end-user programming.

</details>


### [185] [Meandering Photo Lines: Fluid Multiscale Exploration of Photographic Archives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23769)
*Mark-Jan Bludau,Christian Tominski,Marian Dörk*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为"蜿蜒照片线"的可视化方法，将照片集合的空间和时间结构交织成可探索的路径，支持用户从多尺度分析到偶然发现的照片浏览体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有照片浏览界面通常将空间和时间分开使用，缺乏一种整合的视觉形式来呈现摄影旅程本身，尤其是个体摄影师档案中独特的生活轨迹（时空轨迹）为作品提供了必要的背景和意义。

Method: 受河流蜿蜒形态、流畅交互和可折叠可视化启发，设计了两套互补视图：网格状照片聚类视图提供分析性概览，蜿蜒时空曲线视图支持偶然性和反思性探索，并通过动画变换实现两个视图之间的无缝多尺度导航。

Result: 通过两个案例（约10万张全球犹太侨民档案和约2万张个人手机照片）进行了验证，并通过有声思维研究获得了定性反馈。

Conclusion: 该方法能够同时支持分析性审视和好奇心驱动的探索，为照片浏览提供了时空整合的新范式。

Abstract: We introduce a visualization that interweaves spatial and temporal structures of photo collections into explorable pathways. Existing photo browsing interfaces typically use space and time separately for sorting, filtering, or mapping photos. What remains missing is an integrated visual form that represents the photographic journey itself. This is particularly relevant for the individual photographer's archive, where their unique lifeline is written into the collection, and it is this spatiotemporal trajectory that gives necessary context and meaning to the work. Inspired by the winding nature of rivers, fluid interaction, and un/foldable visualizations, Meandering Photo Lines visualizes adjustable pathways through a photo collection across space, time, and topics. Two complementary views enable visual exploration at different scales: A grid-based arrangement of photo clusters offers a more analytical overview, while meandering spatiotemporal curves facilitate more serendipitous and reflective exploration. Animated transformations connect these two views seamlessly for multiscale navigation from spatiotemporal cluster signatures down to chronological photo series and individual photographs. We demonstrate our approach through two case studies: an archive of approximately 100,000 photographs documenting the Jewish diaspora life worldwide from 1978 to 2021 and a personal smartphone collection of around 20,000 pictures. Qualitative feedback from a think-aloud study indicates that our approach supports both analytical examination and curiosity-driven engagement.

</details>


### [186] [Visible to the Court: How AI Is (and Isn't) Litigated in U.S. Federal Court Opinions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23888)
*Julie Yu,Rock Yuren Pang,Jevan Hutson,Katharina Reinecke*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过对559起美国联邦法院AI相关案件的系统梳理，揭示了AI诉讼的七大争议领域、六类技术类型和四类常见诉讼主体，发现司法系统主要依赖既有法律原则进行“碎片化”治理，导致AI风险覆盖不全面，部分AI损害无法得到有效救济。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在联邦层面缺乏AI监管法规的背景下，法院成为AI相关争议的主要解决场所，但此前缺乏对AI诉讼格局的系统性实证研究，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 对559份美国联邦法院意见书中涉及AI的案件进行系统性审查，对争议主题、AI技术类型和涉案当事人进行分类学分析，并与AI事件数据库（AI Incident Database）进行对比。

Result: 识别出七大争议领域、六类AI技术、四类常见诉讼主体及相关法律原则；发现法院记录与AI事件数据库之间存在显著覆盖差距；法院主要依赖既有法律原则而非制定AI专门法律，形成“碎片化”治理模式。

Conclusion: 联邦法院的裁决结果更多取决于现有法律能否“认知”某种损害，而非AI实际造成损害的程度，导致部分AI损害无法获得司法救济。

Abstract: In the United States, artificial intelligence (AI) is rapidly deployed amid limited federal regulation. With courts become a recurring forum in which AI-related practices are scrutinized, it is important to empirically understand the AI litigation landscape to date. We address this gap through a systematic review of 559 U.S. federal court opinions in which AI plays a role in the parties' contentions, taxonomizing (1) common topics of dispute, (2) the AI technologies implicated, and (3) the parties involved, including common plaintiff and defendant types. We identify seven recurring dispute areas, six categories of AI technologies at the center of litigation, and four types of common litigants, alongside legal doctrines used by the litigants. A comparison of this taxonomy to the AI Incident Database revealed substantial gaps in coverage, definitions, and prevalence between documented and litigated harms, suggesting courts capture only part of the AI risk landscape. In addition, we found that court decisions primarily rely on pre-existing legal doctrines to manage AI rather than making new AI-specific laws, producing a form of "piecemeal" AI governance. As a result, federal court outcomes are shaped less by where AI has caused harms and more by which harms are cognizable under existing statutes, leading to certain AI harms remaining unresolved.

</details>


### [187] [Evaluating Closed-Loop EEG Feedback for Simulated Prosthetic Vision in Immersive VR: A Sham-Controlled Feasibility Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23889)
*Ruyi Cao,Lily M. Turkstra,Adyah Rastogi,Michael Beyeler*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究开发了基于EEG的神经自适应训练平台用于模拟假体视觉，但在22名参与者的物体定位任务中，EEG反馈并未带来显著的组间行为表现差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉假体用户需要通过主动视觉搜索来理解稀疏且失真的人工感知，现有训练方法效果有限，因此探索神经反馈训练的可行性。

Method: 在沉浸式虚拟现实中构建了低分辨率光幻视模拟场景，22名视力正常参与者使用干电极EEG头戴设备进行搜索任务。实验组基于EEG参与度指数(β/(α+θ))提供反馈，对照组使用非随机的假反馈。

Result: 两组在定位准确率、完成时间、工作负荷及目标指数调节方面均无显著组间差异，但都表现出组内学习效应。个体差异较大，未发现EEG反馈与行为表现的特异性关联。

Conclusion: 研究证明了将EEG反馈与沉浸式模拟假体视觉集成的可行性，但也揭示了EEG测量指标、单次训练范式及事后反馈设计的局限性。

Abstract: Visual prostheses require users to interpret sparse and distorted artificial percepts through active visual search. We developed an EEG-guided neuroadaptive training platform for simulated prosthetic vision in immersive virtual reality and evaluated its feasibility in a sham-controlled object-localization task. Twenty-two sighted participants searched a virtual desk scene rendered through a low-resolution phosphene simulation while EEG was recorded using a dry-electrode headset integrated with a head-mounted display. During training, participants received post-trial visual feedback based either on a commonly used EEG engagement index, $β/(α+θ)$, or on visually matched non-contingent sham values. Both groups showed comparable within-session improvements in localization performance, consistent with practice, increasing familiarity with the simulated percepts, or refinement of search strategies. EEG-contingent feedback did not produce reliable group-level benefits in localization accuracy, completion time, workload, or modulation of the targeted index. Exploratory analyses showed substantial individual variability, but did not establish a feedback-specific relationship between the engagement index and behavioral performance. These findings demonstrate the feasibility of integrating EEG-contingent feedback with immersive simulated prosthetic vision, while identifying important limitations of the EEG measure, single-session training protocol, and post-trial feedback design.

</details>


### [188] [SHARE: Towards Head-Mounted AR with User-Centric SLAM in Shared Human-Robot Workspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23901)
*Tianyuan Du,Tianyi Hu,Hanting Ye,Maria Gorlatova*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: SHARE是一个用户优先的SLAM系统，通过差异化处理AR用户和机器人代理的延迟需求，在保持机器人跟踪精度的同时，将AR用户平均延迟降低43.3%至13.22毫秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体SLAM系统对所有代理统一处理，忽略了AR用户和机器人在延迟需求上的本质差异，导致AR交互体验出现高延迟问题。

Method: SHARE构建了首个面向人机协作代理的体验模型，自适应调整传输优先级；利用共享工作空间中视觉特征的冗余性，减少边缘计算时间。

Result: 在商业AR头显和地面机器人上部署，AR用户平均延迟13.22毫秒（降低43.3%），跟踪精度保持在2厘米以内，用户研究显示统计显著的感知改善。

Conclusion: 差异化的资源分配策略能有效兼顾AR交互的实时性需求和机器人的跟踪精度，为用户优先的SLAM设计提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Human-Robot Collaboration (HRC) in shared physical spaces using Augmented Reality (AR) interfaces is powered by Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM). Existing multi-agent SLAM systems rely on an edge server to combine visual findings of multiple resource-constrained agents, perform computation, and schedule updates to their local maps. However, the edge treats all agents uniformly and ignores the fundamentally different latency requirements of heterogeneous HRC agents: robots and head-mounted AR users. This uniform resource allocation often results in high lag for user manipulation, as it does not meet the stringent latency requirements of AR. In this work, we design, implement, and evaluate SHARE, a user-centric SLAM system that strategically prioritizes AR user experience while maintaining accurate tracking performance for robots. SHARE builds a first-of-its-kind experience model for HRC agents and adaptively adjusts transmission priorities to match it. To reduce end-to-end latency, SHARE leverages the redundancy of visual features acquired by agents in shared human-robot workspaces to reduce computation time induced by edge-based processing. Real-world deployment with commercial AR headsets and a ground robot achieves 13.22 ms average latency for AR users (43.3% reduction from baseline) while maintaining sub-2-centimeter tracking accuracy. User studies further reveal statistically significant improvements in user perception.

</details>


### [189] [A Sustainable Remote Access Architecture for Digital Inclusion through the Reuse of Discredited TV-BOX Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23935)
*Italo Thiago Felix dos Santos,Carlos Eduardo Correa Queiroz,Adevan Neves Santos,Edgard Luciano Oliveira da Silva*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过回收废弃电子设备（尤其是被没收的TV Box设备）构建低成本、可持续的桌面虚拟化基础设施（VDI）方案，用于资源有限的教育机构，并验证其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字包容与电子废弃物增加是重大社会挑战，资源有限的教育机构在扩展和维护计算机实验室方面面临困难，亟需低成本、可持续的解决方案。

Method: 采用翻新的Linux服务器作为后端，将原本要被处理的TV Box设备改装为ARM架构的瘦客户端（安装兼容Linux发行版），构建完整的桌面虚拟化基础设施。

Result: 该方案被证明是支持数字教育倡议的可行替代方案，能够在降低部署成本的同时实现硬件再利用。

Conclusion: 该VDI方案符合循环经济原则，通过硬件复用有效降低了教育计算环境的部署成本，为可持续的数字教育提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Digital inclusion and the volume of electronic waste (e-waste) are major challenges for society, with direct impacts on the educational context. Educational institutions, especially those with limited resources, face difficulties in expanding and maintaining computer laboratories. This paper presents the implementation of a sustainable, low-cost Desktop Virtualization Infrastructure (VDI) developed through the reuse of discarded electronic equipment. The proposed solution employs a refurbished Linux server and repurposes confiscated TV Box devices (originally intended for disposal) into functional ARM-based thin clients by installing a compatible Linux distribution. This approach is aligned with the principles of the circular economy by promoting hardware reuse and seeks to reduce the costs associated with deploying computing environments for education. The paper describes the system architecture, the device repurposing process, and the observed results, indicating that this approach represents a viable alternative to support digital education initiatives with a focus on sustainability.

</details>


### [190] [A Design Space for Quantum Circuit Visualizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24042)
*Hyeok Kim,Leilani Battle*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文分析了182个静态和12个交互式量子电路可视化案例，提出了统一的设计空间，以支持跨平台的量子电路可视化增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子电路可视化工具因平台差异而编码方式各异，缺乏统一的语法标准，阻碍了跨平台的一致性和社区协作。

Method: 通过收集并分析182个静态和12个交互式量子电路可视化案例（来自在线教程、文献、演示文稿和现有系统），构建了设计空间。

Result: 建立了量子电路可视化的设计空间，揭示了其与现有可视化原则的关联及独特方面。

Conclusion: 未来量子电路可视化系统应在数据结构、认知理解和系统可集成性三个方向进行探索。

Abstract: Quantum circuit visualizations play an essential role in supporting sense-making and communication of quantum programs. While several tools exist for rendering quantum circuits, they vary widely in encoding options due to idiosyncrasies among machine and platform providers. We observe an opportunity to coalesce these disparate rendering approaches under a single, unified grammar to enable consistent, cross-platform enhancement of quantum circuit visualizations. However, it is unclear how to design such a grammar to best support the quantum computing community. Towards this end, we contribute a design space of quantum circuit visualizations by analyzing 182 static and 12 interactive cases collected from online tutorials and documentations, research publications, public presentations, and prior systems. Based on our analysis, we discuss how our design space relates to existing visualization principles yet exhibits unique aspects. We conclude with opportunities for future systems regarding data structure, cognition, and integrability.

</details>


### [191] [The Effect of Photorealism Consistency between the Virtual Hands and Environment on the Sense of Body Ownership and Presence in Virtual Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24047)
*Nami Ogawa,Takuji Narumi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过远程VR实验(N=117)探究了虚拟手部渲染风格（真实感）对身体所有权感的影响，发现渲染风格及其与环境的一致性均不影响身体所有权感，但虚拟环境的真实感越强，临场感越强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究探讨了视觉真实感中拟人化维度对身体所有权感的影响，但照片级真实感（photorealism）这一维度的作用尚未被系统考察。此外，照片级真实感对临场感的影响在现有研究中证据不一致，需要进一步厘清。

Method: 采用3×3混合设计的远程VR实验（N=117），操纵虚拟手部与环境两者的渲染风格，通过问卷收集主观数据进行分析。

Result: 虚拟手部的渲染风格及其与环境的风格一致性均不影响身体所有权感；但虚拟环境的照片级真实感越强，用户的临场感越强。

Conclusion: 虚拟身体真实感的不同维度对身体所有权感的影响存在差异，需要采用合适的真实感分类框架来更深入地理解虚拟身体的所有权错觉机制。

Abstract: Virtual reality (VR) technology allows users to feel a virtual body as if it was their own (i.e., body ownership illusion). Previous studies have explored how the visual realism of the user's virtual body influences the sense of body ownership in VR, focusing on the aspect of anthropomorphism. However, the effect of photorealism, another element that characterizes the visual realism of a virtual human, has not been systematically examined in the context of body ownership illusion. Therefore, we investigate the effect of the rendering style of virtual hands on the sense of body ownership, hypothesizing that the effect is affected by the rendering style of the virtual environment. In addition, we examine the effect of photorealism on presence (i.e., the sense of being there), as existing studies have offered inconsistent evidence. To this aim, we conducted a 3 x 3 mixed-design remote VR experiment (N=117) that factored in the rendering styles of both the virtual hand and the environment and analyzed the subjective data from the questionnaire. The results suggest that neither the rendering style of virtual hands nor the consistency of the rendering style between the virtual hand and the environment influenced the sense of body ownership. Nevertheless, the more photorealistically the virtual environment was rendered, the stronger the presence. Our results indicate that different dimensions of virtual body realism affect the sense of body ownership differently, thereby highlighting the importance of applying a suitable classification of realism to understand the body ownership illusion for virtual bodies more in depth.

</details>


### [192] [Towards simultaneous decoding of kinetic and kinematic movement parameters during grasp and lift task by noninvasive brain imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24081)
*Parth G. Dangi,Yogesh Kumaar Meena*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了三种回归模型（偏最小二乘回归、多层感知机、注意力回归器）从EEG信号解码多个运动参数，其中注意力回归器在多参数同步解码中表现最佳（R²=0.8，延迟29.2ms），但单参数解码性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑机接口可辅助行动受限者，但难点在于通过准确解码多个运动学与动力学参数来扩展其可用性和控制能力。

Method: 采用偏最小二乘回归、多层感知机和注意力回归器三种模型，在WAY EEG GAL数据集上评估，对比了特定受试者与独立受试者条件，以及单一模型和独立模型两种策略。

Result: 注意力回归器在多参数同步解码中R²=0.8，延迟29.2ms；多层感知机R²=0.49；注意力模型在单参数解码中性能下降。

Conclusion: 注意力模型在实时多命令脑机接口系统中具有潜力，有助于开发更直观的控制设备。

Abstract: Brain-machine interfaces (BMIs) can assist individuals with limited mobility, such as stroke survivors or amputees. One of the key challenges in developing BMIs is expanding their usability and control, which can be achieved by accurately decoding multiple kinematic and kinetic parameters. To address this, we propose three regression models: partial least squares regressor, multilayered perceptron, and attention based regressor, to decode multiple movement parameters from EEG signals. We evaluated these models on the WAY EEG GAL dataset, focusing on their performance under subject specific and subject independent conditions with two strategies: a single model for all parameters and a baseline with separate models for each parameter. Among all regressors, the attention based regressor achieved the best performance, with an $R^2$ of 0.8 and a latency of 29.2 milliseconds, demonstrating significant improvement in simultaneous multi parameter decoding. However, its performance dropped for single parameter decoding. The multi layered perceptron showed more consistent but lower accuracy across both decoding types ($R^2$ = 0.49). These findings highlight the potential of attention based models for real time multi command BMI systems and contribute to the development of more intuitive control devices.

</details>


### [193] [EEGForceFusion: Joint Tokenised-Continuous Representation Learning for Subject-Independent Grasp Force Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24126)
*Sankalp Sunil Turankar,Yogesh Kumar Meena*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出一种混合连续-离散化表示的EEG解码框架，融合卷积循环网络、量化分词和Transformer时序建模，在WAY-EEG-GAL数据集上实现离线R²=0.817和实时R²=0.793的抓握力解码性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在连续抓握力解码中面临复杂时间动态、高个体差异和泛化能力不足的问题。

Method: 混合EEG解码框架：联合建模连续表示和离散化（量化分词）表示，集成卷积循环表示学习、量化分词和Transformer时序建模，采用统一融合回归架构。

Result: 在WAY-EEG-GAL数据集上，严格留一法交叉验证下，离线R²=0.817，模拟实时评估R²=0.793，延迟满足实时应用要求。

Conclusion: 混合连续-离散化表示方法能有效提升EEG抓握力解码的跨被试泛化能力和实时性，适用于辅助机器人、神经康复和人机交互。

Abstract: Brain-machine interfaces provide a link between neural activity and external devices, enabling restoration of motor function and advancing human-machine interaction using non-invasive electroencephalography (EEG). However, continuous grasp force decoding remains challenging due to complex temporal dynamics, high inter-subject variability, and limited generalisation of existing approaches. To address this, we propose a hybrid EEG decoding framework that jointly models continuous and tokenised representations, enabling capture of both fine-grained neural structure and long-range temporal dependencies. The proposed approach integrates convolutional-recurrent representation learning, quantisation-based tokenisation, and transformer-based temporal modelling within a unified fusion-based regression architecture. Experimental evaluation on the WAY-EEG-GAL dataset under strict leave-one-subject-out conditions achieves $R^2$ = 0.817 in offline settings and $R^2$ = 0.793 in simulated real-time evaluation, with latency suitable for real-time deployment. These results demonstrate strong cross-subject generalisation and highlight the practicality of hybrid continuous-tokenised representations for real-time EEG-based force decoding in assistive robotics, neuro-rehabilitation, and human-machine interaction.

</details>


### [194] [Order-Bound Companionship: The Practice of Emotional Labor in Professional Game Companionship](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24363)
*Xiaohe Mo,Yujie Zhang,Yizhen Li,Jianyi Wang,Qinyi Liao,Ray LC*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究聚焦中国平台经济中的游戏陪玩这一数字劳动实践，通过对22名从业者的访谈，发现陪玩者采用"订单导向"机制：在付费服务中进行沉浸式深层表演，订单结束后则完全情感抽离。研究还识别出以陪玩者为中心的三角网络，以及从业者频繁在陪玩者、客户和俱乐部运营者之间进行角色转换的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在平台零工经济中，涉及情感投入的关系型服务日益增多，但游戏陪玩这一数字劳动实践尚未得到充分研究。本研究立足于中国独特的社会文化和多平台背景，试图从微观层面解析这一新兴劳动形式中的情感边界管理和利益相关者网络。

Method: 采用质性研究方法，对22名游戏陪玩从业者进行深度访谈，从微观视角分析关系型零工工作中劳动者如何管理亲密边界和利益相关者网络。

Result: 发现了"订单导向"机制——陪玩者在付费服务中执行沉浸式深层表演，订单结束后则完全情感抽离。识别出以陪玩者为中心的三角网络：与客户的情境化表演、竞争共生型同伴关系、与陪玩俱乐部的相互依赖治理。同时发现从业者存在显著的身份流动性，频繁在陪玩者、客户和俱乐部运营者之间转换角色。

Conclusion: 研究为未来亲密型数字劳动的治理和平台设计提供了启示，强调需要明确考虑制度化的边界以及零工工作者不断变化的心理需求。

Abstract: Labor in platform gig economy increasingly involves services involving relationship that demand significant emotional investment. Grounded in China's unique socio-cultural and multi-platform context, this study explores professional game companionship, an under-explored digital labor practice. Through interviews with 22 game companionship practitioners, we used a micro-level perspective to relational gig work to analyze how workers navigate intimate boundaries and stakeholder networks. We found that companions adopt an "order-bound" mechanism: performing immersive deep acting during paid sessions, followed by complete emotional disengagement post-order. We also identified a tripartite companion-centric network featuring scenario-based performances with clients, competitive-symbiotic peer relations, and interdependent governance with companionship clubs. Furthermore, significant identity fluidity exists, with individuals frequently transitioning between companion, client, and club operator roles. We provide implications for future labor governance and platform designs for intimate digital work that explicitly account for institutionalized boundaries and gig workers' shifting psychological needs.

</details>


### [195] [Let Me Look at You: Advanced Facial Expression Modeling for Conversational Speech Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24430)
*Yifan Hu,Shuwei He,Rui Liu,Haizhou Li*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出FacialTalker，一种基于大语言模型、融合面部表情感知的对话语音合成框架。通过AUTokenizer将面部表情离散化为紧凑token，并引入双直接偏好优化（DualDPO）策略同时约束视觉和语音序列。构建了大规模多模态对话数据集VSDD-1K（1,033小时）。实验表明该方法在表情感知和语音合成质量上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话语音合成方法忽视面部表情这一重要模态（面部表情编码了微妙的、丰富的情感线索），且缺乏大规模自然对话的语音-视觉多模态数据集，限制了对话场景下视觉情感理解的发展。

Method: 1. 提出AUTokenizer：基于面部动作单元（Action Units）监督训练的单码本视觉分词器，将每帧面部表情离散化为紧凑token。2. 提出DualDPO策略：扩展直接偏好优化（DPO），同时对视觉和语音token序列施加偏好约束。3. 构建VSDD-1K数据集：通过全自动流水线从真实互联网对话收集，含1,033小时同步说话人视频和语音，85%以上帧含有效人脸。

Result: 在客观和主观实验上，FacialTalker在面部表情感知和语音合成质量上一致优于强基线，生成的语音更自然、更具表现力、更符合对话语境。同时验证了训练策略和数据集构建管线的有效性。

Conclusion: 融合面部表情模态能够显著提升对话语音合成的自然度和表现力；提出的AUTokenizer、DualDPO策略以及VSDD-1K数据集均为该领域提供了有效贡献。

Abstract: Conversational Speech Synthesis is a fundamental component of human-computer interaction, aiming to generate contextually appropriate, expressive, and empathetic speech. However, facial expressions encode subtle and rich affective cues that are crucial for empathetic speech interaction, whereas existing approaches often overlook this important modality. In addition, the lack of large-scale natural conversational datasets with both speech and visual modalities also limits the development of visual affect understanding in conversational settings.To address these limitations, we propose FacialTalker, a facial-expression-aware CSS framework built upon a large language model backbone. To efficiently encode facial expressions, we propose AUTokenizer, a single-codebook visual tokenizer that discretizes each frame-level facial expression into a compact token, trained with supervision from combinations of facial Action Units. We further introduce a dual direct preference optimization (DualDPO) strategy, which extends the DPO by jointly imposing preference constraints on both visual and speech token sequences, to enhance the model's understanding of facial expressions and speech semantics in multimodal conversational contexts. Moreover, we construct VSDD-1K, a large-scale multimodal dialogue dataset collected through a fully automated pipeline from real-world Internet conversations, comprising over 1,033 hours of synchronized speaker videos and speech, with more than 85\% of frames containing valid faces. Extensive objective and subjective experiments demonstrate that FacialTalker consistently outperforms strong baselines in facial-expression perception and speech synthesis quality, generating speech that is more natural, expressive, and better aligned with the conversational context. The results also validate the effectiveness of our training strategy and dataset construction pipeline.

</details>


### [196] [RemiAssist: A Therapist-Supporting System for Photo-Based Reminiscence Therapy in Dementia Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24536)
*Shuchang Xu,Minglong Tang,Junyan Mao,Xiaofu Jin,Wazeer Zulfikar,Yasith Samaradivakara,Jiayi Zhou,Huamin Qu,Yuling Sun,Pattie Maes*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: RemiAssist是一个支持治疗师参与AI辅助规划与实时引导的怀旧疗法系统，通过记忆图和情境感知引导策略，在8对治疗师-痴呆症患者实地研究中实现了44%的规划效率提升和54%的对话时长增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于AI的照片怀旧疗法（PRT）系统主要关注患者与AI的交互，忽视了治疗师在实际PRT实施中的关键作用。本研究旨在将治疗师纳入循环，通过AI辅助增强治疗师的规划和引导能力。

Method: 提出了两个核心技术：(1) 记忆图（Memory Graph），将患者的照片集合中的关键生活事件组织为层次化图结构，支持以主题为中心的干预规划；(2) 情境感知引导策略（Context-Aware Guiding Strategy），实时提供建议，帮助治疗师引导怀旧对话并应对敏感情境。通过8对治疗师-患者配对进行实地研究验证。

Result: 规划效率提升44%，对话时长增加54%，并在处理敏感情境时提供了及时支持。

Conclusion: AI系统有机会赋能治疗师，在痴呆症护理中实现更个性化的怀旧疗法。

Abstract: Despite growing interest in applying AI to photo-based reminiscence therapy (PRT) for people with dementia (PwD), existing systems primarily focus on PwD-AI interaction and often overlook therapists' critical role in practical PRT delivery. We present RemiAssist, a system that supports therapist-in-the-loop PRT through AI-assisted planning and real-time facilitation. RemiAssist incorporates two core techniques: (1) a Memory Graph, which organizes key life events from a PwD's photo collection into a hierarchical graph to support theme-centered intervention planning; and (2) a Context-Aware Guiding Strategy, which provides real-time suggestions to help therapists guide reminiscence conversations and respond to sensitive situations. A field study with eight therapist-PwD dyads suggests that RemiAssist was associated with a 44% improvement in planning efficiency and a 54% increase in conversation duration, and provided timely support for handling sensitive situations. We highlight opportunities for AI systems to empower therapists and enable more personalized reminiscence therapy in dementia care.

</details>


### [197] [Proceedings of The First Reflection in Creative Experience (RiCE) Workshop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24558)
*Corey Ford,Olga Sutskova,Samuel Rhys Cox,Sarah Sterman,Max Kreminski,Rosa Van Koningsbruggen,Anqi Wang,Ege Otenen,Karly Ross,Giulia Di Fede,Yinmiao Li,Salvatore Andolina,Marit Bentvelzen,Pan Hui,Nick Bryan-Kinns*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研讨会首次汇聚HCI、认知科学、设计、AI、学习科学和数字艺术等多领域研究者，探讨创意交互中的反思与元认知问题，旨在建立反思在创意交互中的分类体系，指导未来创意实践和工具开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HCI研究对反思的关注集中于任务导向的明确目标（如反思个人数据或教学成果），而创意用户体验中的反思具有开放性和难以言喻的特点，缺乏针对性研究。

Method: 通过跨学科研讨会形式，汇集HCI、认知科学、设计、AI、学习科学和数字艺术等领域的研究者、设计师、教育者和艺术家，基于前期与HCI研究者和艺术家的讨论，围绕三个主题展开：捕捉创意情境中的反思、利用艺术支持反思以实现伦理变革、设计增强批判性思维的创意AI。

Result: 形成一套指导性的创意交互反思分类体系（taxonomy），为未来的创意实践和工具开发提供参考框架。

Conclusion: 通过跨学科对话，将反思研究从任务导向拓展到创意交互领域，弥合现有研究空白，为面向创意体验的反思性工具和系统设计奠定理论基础。

Abstract: Reflection and metacognition are central to the creative user experience. However, most HCI research on reflection focuses on clear, task-oriented goals such as to reflect on personal data or pedagogical outcomes. This contrasts with the open-ended and challenging to articulate goals of creative user experiences. For the first time, this workshop brings together interdisciplinary researchers, designers, educators, and artists across HCI, Cognitive Science, Design, AI, Learning Sciences, and Digital Art to examine reflection in creative interaction. The workshop will discuss themes, drawn from earlier discussions with HCI researchers and artists, on: how best to capture reflection in creative contexts, how to leverage the arts to support reflection for ethical change, and how to design creative AI that enhances - not hinders - critical thinking. By bringing interdisciplinary perspectives on reflection into discussion, the workshop will develop a guiding taxonomy for reflection in creative interaction to inform future creative practice and tool development.

</details>


### [198] [Designing Within the Lines: Practitioners' Perspectives and Visualisation Tool Evaluation in the Arabic Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24571)
*Muna Alebri,Noëlle Rakotondravony,Yassine Bechqito,Georgia Panagiotidou,Lane Harrison,Hassan Aldhanhabi,Salem Alkaabi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文研究了阿拉伯语可视化从业者在从右到左（RTL）脚本环境下的设计实践，发现他们不得不在阿拉伯语阅读习惯、普遍的从左到右（LTR）视觉规范以及有限工具支持之间不断进行权衡，并付出了大量额外劳动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可视化设计指南和最佳实践主要面向英语和LTR脚本用户，忽视了超过20亿阿拉伯语脚本用户的需求，缺乏对语言、工具支持和文化背景如何影响设计决策的研究。

Method: 对7款主流基于GUI的可视化创作工具进行阿拉伯数据集分析评估，并对11位来自新闻、设计和数据分析领域的阿拉伯语从业者进行访谈。

Result: 可视化从业者经常在阿拉伯语阅读惯例与"普遍"LTR视觉规范之间产生张力；工具对RTL镜像支持不一致、数字渲染差、地图默认编码地缘政治假设；从业者需手动镜像图表、修复对齐问题、使用多工具工作流，并在语言选择、交互性和图表类型上做出战略妥协。

Conclusion: RTL可视化工作是在多种约束下进行的，影响了从业者的能动性和创造力；可视化工具和默认设置操作化了RTL语境下的语言和地缘政治权力；需要研究方向与设计启示以更好地支持RTL从业者。

Abstract: Design guidelines and best practices serve as references that support designers throughout the visualisation design process. While considerable effort has identified the elements that contribute to effective data visualisations, little attention has been paid to how language (scripts and reading direction), tool support, and cultural context also shape design decisions. As a result, assumptions of homogeneity persist, with visualisation practices predominantly benefiting users of English and left-to-right (LTR) scripts while overlooking the needs of over two billion Arabic script users. We investigate how Arabic-speaking visualisation practitioners design for right-to-left (RTL) scripts. We report on an analytical evaluation of seven popular GUI-based visualisation authoring tools using an Arabic dataset complemented by interviews with 11 Arabic-speaking practitioners across journalism, design, and data analysis. Our findings reveal that visualisation practitioners constantly negotiate tensions between Arabic reading conventions, "universal" LTR visual norms, and limited tool support for Arabic text, Eastern numerals, and maps. They engage in substantial labour, such as manually mirroring charts, fixing alignment issues, and stitching together multi-tool workflows, while making strategic compromises in language choice, interactivity, and chart type. Our tool analysis further reveals fragmented, inconsistent support for RTL mirroring, poor numeral rendering, and map defaults that encode geopolitical assumptions. In light of these findings, we discuss how RTL visualisation work is carried out under many constraints that affect agency and creativity. We argue that visualisation tools and defaults operationalise linguistic and geopolitical power in RTL contexts, and offer research directions and design implications that more robustly support RTL practitioners.

</details>


### [199] [Characterizing In-the-Wild Personal Listening Device Use to Inform Earable Application Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24603)
*Supraja Ramesh,Jonas Hummel,Silvia Becker,Christopher Clarke,Jake Stuchbury-Wass,Kai Kunze,Axel Loewe,Michael Beigl,Tobias Röddiger*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过330人问卷调查和90人Apple Health数据，发现耳戴设备日均使用从2020年37分钟增至2024年64分钟，使用呈间歇性特征（48%天数无使用），高音量暴露仅占4%周数，并据此提出耳戴计算应基于真实使用模式而非假设进行设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耳戴设备正从音频播放工具转变为健康、交互和情境感知的传感平台，但现有系统设计和评估通常基于对人们佩戴时长、频率和情境的强假设，缺乏真实的野外行为数据支撑。

Method: 结合330名成年人的问卷调查与其中90人通过Apple Health捐赠的多年被动记录的头戴式音频暴露数据，分析PLD使用的地点、时间、近年变化趋势以及心理特质和社会情境与使用的关联。

Result: 日均使用从2020年37分钟增至2024年64分钟；48%的天数无使用记录；周末会话更少但更长；感觉寻求（尤其是去抑制）与自我报告使用呈小到中等正相关；年轻成人音量更高；仅4%的周数超过WHO安全听力限值；多数人避免在社交场合使用PLD。

Conclusion: 耳戴计算应建立在对间歇性佩戴、系统性情境空白、针对性安全听力干预以及基于心理社会和人口统计特征的个性化设计等真实使用模式的基础上。

Abstract: Ear-worn devices are evolving from audio-playback tools into sensing platforms for health, interaction, and context-awareness. Yet, earable systems are typically designed and evaluated under strong assumptions about how long, how often, and in which situations people actually wear personal listening devices (PLDs). To ground these assumptions in-the-wild behavior, we combine a survey of 330 adults with multi-year, passively logged headphone audio-exposure records donated via Apple Health by 90 of them. We characterize where and when people use PLDs, how logged use has changed in recent years, and how psychological traits and social context associate with PLD usage. Our results show that logged mean daily use has increased from 37 minutes in 2020 to 64 minutes in 2024. Listening was intermittent: no listening was logged on 48% of participant-days in 2024, and sessions were fewer but longer on weekends. Sensation seeking, particularly disinhibition, showed small to medium positive associations with self-reported PLD use. Younger adults listened at higher volumes than the 25-34 group. High-volume exposure was uncommon, with only 4% of participant-weeks exceeding World Health Organization (WHO) safe-listening limits. Finally, most participants also reported avoiding PLD use in social situations. We translate these findings into implications for earable computing: realistic expectations of intermittent rather than continuous wear, contextual coverage that anticipates systematic gaps, targeted safe-listening interventions, and personalization grounded in psychosocial and demographic profiles rather than assumptions of uniform use.

</details>


### [200] [Leveling the Playing Field: Temporal Video Segmentation for Individuals with ADHD in Computing Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24612)
*Veronica Pimenova,Chris Lee,Baramee Bhakdibhumi,Simon Chu,Andrew Begel*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 针对ADHD学习者的教学视频分段干预（单指令+固定暂停）能显著降低认知负荷，使ADHD学习者的表现接近非ADHD学习者，符合UDL目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ADHD患者在异步学习环境中面临高认知负荷障碍，现有教学视频缺乏对ADHD神经认知特征的适配。

Method: 后处理视频分段干预（单指令块+固定暂停），17名ADHD与10名非ADHD被试的组内对照实验。

Result: 干预对所有参与者均有提升，但对ADHD组效果更显著，将其错误率和犹豫降至与非ADHD组相当的水平。

Conclusion: 认知适配的后处理视频修改能有效缩小不同神经认知特征群体的表现差距，支持通用学习设计（UDL）理念。

Abstract: Individuals with Attention-Deficit/Hyperactivity Disorder (ADHD) often face significant barriers in computing education. In asynchronous learning environments, instructional videos can impose high extraneous cognitive load, often relying on assumptions about sustained attention and working memory that do not align with ADHD neurocognitive profiles. In this work, we evaluate a post-hoc video processing intervention that segments instructional content into single-instruction chunks followed by fixed-length pauses to reduce cognitive load. In a within-participants controlled study with 17 individuals with ADHD and 10 without, we find that the intervention has an equalizing effect. Although it improved performance for all participants, gains were larger for those with ADHD, reducing their errors and hesitations to levels comparable to those of participants without ADHD under the same intervention. These results align with the goals of Universal Design for Learning (UDL), by showing that cognitively-aligned, post-hoc instructional video modifications can reduce performance disparities across diverse neurocognitive profiles.

</details>


### [201] [Make or Take: How Students Navigate Self-Created and Instructor-Provided Cheat Sheets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24736)
*Helen Weixu Chen,Victoria Sakhnini,Lesley Istead*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 关于学生在考试中如何选择自制与教师提供的小抄，以及这种选择与备考方式的关系的纵向研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对学生选择自制或教师提供小抄的决策过程及其与备考策略关系的深入理解。

Method: 在软件需求课程中进行纵向研究，收集三个调查波次的数据（53、50、44份回应），41名学生完成全部三次调查形成纵向队列。

Result: 学生的选择受多种因素影响：对教师专业知识的信任、个性化需求、备考效率。学生的态度随时间变化，偏好体现在小抄使用模式、内容覆盖感知和考试挑战中。

Conclusion: 学生的选择受多种因素影响且态度随时间演变，为考试中小抄的设计与使用政策提供了实证依据。

Abstract: The use of cheat sheets in exams is often framed as a way to reduce cognitive load and support student performance. However, little is known about how students choose between self-created and instructor-provided cheat sheets, or how these choices relate to their broader approaches to exam preparation. We conducted a longitudinal study in a senior-level undergraduate software requirements course, where students could use either an instructor-provided or a self-created cheat sheet for both the midterm and final exams. Across three survey waves, we received 53, 50, and 44 responses, respectively. 41 students completed all three surveys and formed the longitudinal cohort used to examine how choices and experiences evolved over time, while exam-specific analyses used all available responses from the corresponding wave. Our findings identify several considerations that shaped students' choices, including trust in instructor expertise, the desire for personalization, and preparation efficiency. We further show how students' attitudes shifted over time and how their preferences were reflected in patterns of cheat sheet use, perceived content coverage, and challenges encountered during the exams.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [202] [Concept-based Visual Counterfactual Explanations with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22544)
*Yassine Oueslati,Daniil Kirilenko,Martin Gjoreski,Marc Langheinrich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出C-VCE框架，通过在扩散模型中内置概念瓶颈层生成视觉反事实解释，无需依赖外部噪声鲁棒分类器，在保持编辑最小化的同时实现预测翻转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的反事实方法依赖外部分类器对噪声图像的稳定判断，导致系统脆弱且难以部署，亟需一种无需外部分类器、更稳健的反事实生成方案。

Method: 将分类器直接构建在生成模型内部，通过概念瓶颈层用可解释语义特征引导反事实；引入概率正则化项平衡预测翻转与图像保真度；使用梯度掩码限制修改区域。

Result: 在CelebA等基准上，C-VCE达到或优于现有方法的翻转率，同时生成的图像在视觉上更接近原图、畸变更少。

Conclusion: 暴露并控制内部概念层是使强大生成模型更易理解和安全使用的有效途径，C-VCE为需要具体"如果-那么"图像的安全关键视觉系统提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Visual counterfactual explanations aim to answer "what minimal change to this image would flip the model's prediction?", and are increasingly important as vision models are deployed in safety-critical domains (e.g., medicine). Existing diffusion-based methods can produce realistic edits, but they rely on external classifiers that must work reliably on noisy images, which makes them fragile and hard to deploy for robust explanations. We introduce C-VCE, a new diffusion framework that builds the classifier directly into the generative model via a concept bottleneck layer, so that counterfactuals are guided by human-interpretable features (concepts) instead of a separate noise robust classifier that works with pixel-level edits. Our model lets users to toggle on/off semantic concepts during sampling, then minimally adjusts relevant image regions, while preserving the rest of the image, respecting feature correlations. To keep edits small and controlled, we add a simple probabilistic regularizer that balances "change the prediction" against "stay close to the original", plus a gradient-based mask that confines modifications to the most relevant regions. On benchmarks such as CelebA, C-VCE matches or improves flip rates while producing counterfactuals that are visually closer to the input and less distorted than baselines that depend on separate noisy-image classifiers. These properties make C-VCE a practical tool for vision systems where users need concrete "what-if" images without having to trust an additional, noise-robust classifier. More broadly, our results suggest that exposing and controlling an internal concept layer is a promising way to make powerful generative models easier to understand and safer to use.

</details>


### [203] [SeT-Diff: Towards Semantic Foundation Models for HPC Telemetry and Time-Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22548)
*Giovanni B. Esposito,Francesco Antici,Daniele Cesarini,Andrea Bartolini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了SeT-Diff，首个面向计算节点遥测时间序列的基础模型，基于扩散机制并利用传感器语义描述进行条件生成，在重构、插补、预测和虚拟传感等任务上表现出色，MAE低至0.033-0.047，且具备零样本排列稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HPC机器学习方法依赖静态固定的传感器变量子集，当目标任务或传感器指标变化时模型失效，缺乏灵活通用的数字孪生方案。

Method: 提出SeT-Diff，一种基于扩散过程的生成式基础模型，将生成过程条件化于每个传感器的语义描述上，从而将系统动力学与数据集结构解耦。

Result: 在真实超算数据集上，重构任务MAE为0.0470；零样本排列稳定性好；单个预训练模型在数据插补、预测和虚拟传感（热推断MAE 0.033）任务上均表现优异。

Conclusion: SeT-Diff是一种高效的数据驱动数字孪生方案，具备灵活性和通用性，适用于HPC系统。

Abstract: Data centers and their compute nodes require accurate and flexible digital twins capable of modeling the complex interplay of workloads, environmental parameters, and physical metrics. Current machine learning approaches for HPC and its telemetry typically rely on a static subset of anonymous, fixed-position sensor variables tailored to single tasks. Consequently, these models become obsolete when target tasks change or sensor metrics vary. We propose SeT-Diff, the first foundational model for compute node telemetry and time-series. Unlike rigid architectures, our diffusion-based approach conditions the generative process on each sensor's semantic description, decoupling the system dynamics from the structure of the dataset. Experiments on a real-world supercomputer dataset demonstrate a Mean Absolute Error (MAE) of 0.0470 on reconstruction tasks. SeT-Diff exhibits zero-shot permutation stability, maintaining accuracy with negligible degradation even when sensors are shuffled. A single pre-trained model effectively performs data imputation, forecasting, and virtual sensing - achieving a 0.033 MAE in thermal inference - making SeT-Diff an effective data-driven digital twin for HPC systems.

</details>


### [204] [QFoldAgent: An Autonomous Quantum Optimization Multi-Agent System for Protein Structure Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22549)
*Winson Chen,Yuqi Zhang,Sixu Chen,Nuo Xu,Qiang Guan,Caiwen Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为QFoldAgent的闭环多智能体框架，用于5残基四面体晶格蛋白质结构预测。通过设计智能体提议序列条件惩罚、VQE量子-经典管道优化哈密顿量、反馈智能体利用能量景观诊断和MolProbity验证信号迭代改进惩罚系数。在QDockBank基准上将中位RMSD从3.64 Å降至3.20 Å，在未见序列上将结构有效性从87.5%提升至98.7%，恢复87%的初始无效案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于晶格的量子-经典蛋白质结构预测工作流通常手动固定哈密顿量惩罚权重，且仅在模拟中评估非常短的片段，缺乏系统性优化惩罚系数的方法。

Method: 提出QFoldAgent闭环多智能体框架，包含三个智能体：设计智能体提出序列条件惩罚，VQE量子-经典管道在Qiskit Aer噪声下优化哈密顿量，反馈智能体利用能量景观诊断和MolProbity验证信号迭代改进惩罚系数。在QDockBank（55个已知结构片段）和覆盖优化未见序列（100个）两个数据集上评估。

Result: QDockBank基准中位RMSD从3.64 Å降至3.20 Å，最难目标提升最大。未见序列结构有效性从87.5%提升至98.7%，87%初始无效案例被恢复。最强控制器在87%序列上改善cycle-3能量，同时维持96% Ramachandran偏好几何。

Conclusion: 迭代智能体控制能够系统性地改善量子5残基设置中的优化行为并减少失败案例。

Abstract: Hybrid quantum-classical protein structure prediction depends strongly on Hamiltonian penalty weights, yet existing lattice-based workflows typically fix these coefficients by hand and evaluate only very short fragments in simulation. We present QFoldAgent, a closed-loop multi-agent framework for 5-residue tetrahedral-lattice folding in which a design agent proposes sequence-conditioned penalties, a VQE-based quantum-classical pipeline optimizes the resulting Hamiltonian under Qiskit Aer noise, and a feedback agent uses energy-landscape diagnostics and MolProbity validation signals to refine penalties across cycles. Ground-truth metrics such as RMSD are never exposed to the agents and are used only for evaluation. We study the framework on two complementary datasets: 55 QDockBank-derived fragments with known structures and 100 coverage-optimized unseen sequences. On the QDockBank benchmark, QFoldAgent reduces median RMSD from 3.64 Å to 3.20 Å, with the largest gains on the hardest targets. On unseen sequences, the closed loop raises structural validity from 87.5% to 98.7%, recovers 87% of initially invalid cases, and the strongest controller improves cycle-3 energy on 87% of sequences while maintaining 96% Ramachandran-favored geometry. These results show that iterative agent control can systematically improve optimization behavior and reduce failure cases in a 5-residue quantum setting.

</details>


### [205] [Same Question, Different Answers: Evaluating LLM Reliability Beyond Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22554)
*Kazem Faghih,Yize Cheng,Shoumik Saha,Mobina Pournemat,Armin Gerami,Soheil Feizi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM在不同但等价的提问方式下，回答经常不一致，单次正确性不能反映真实可靠性；但模型通常至少对一种表述给出正确答案，表明知识存在但检索不稳定，且简单的自我释义策略可部分恢复潜在知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试中LLM准确率看似很高，但模型能否在语义等价的变体问法下稳定应用知识尚不明确，标准准确率指标可能掩盖了实质性的不稳定性。

Method: 在4个基准测试、13个模型上，系统性地研究模型在保持语义不变的释义下，回答事实性问答和数学推理任务时答案的变化情况。

Result: 整体准确率在不同释义间变化不大，但实例层面不稳定：许多问题在不同表述下答案正误交替，不匹配率超23%；单次正确的问题中答案翻转率更高；但大多数问题至少有一种表述能得到正确答案。

Conclusion: 标准准确率指标可能掩盖LLM回答的实质不稳定性，评估等价输入下的一致性才能更清楚地反映模型可靠性；简单的自我释义策略可部分恢复潜在知识，提升推理性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often achieve strong accuracy on benchmarks, yet it remains unclear how reliably they apply this knowledge when the same question is phrased in different but equivalent ways. In this work, we study how model answers change under meaning-preserving paraphrases across factual question answering and mathematical reasoning tasks. Across four benchmarks and 13 models, we find that model outputs frequently depend on the exact wording of the prompt. While overall accuracy typically changes only modestly across paraphrases, instance-level behavior is far less stable: for many questions, models alternate between correct and incorrect answers depending on phrasing, with mismatch rates reaching more than 23%. Conditioning on questions that are answered correctly in their original form reveals even larger failures measured by answer flip rates, showing that single-prompt correctness is often a poor indicator of reliability. At the same time, we find that models often produce a correct answer for at least one paraphrase of a question, suggesting that the underlying knowledge is present but inconsistently retrieved. Building on this observation, we show that a simple self-paraphrasing strategy can partially recover this latent knowledge and improve performance at inference time. Together, these findings suggest that standard accuracy metrics can mask substantial instability, and that evaluating consistency across equivalent inputs provides a clearer picture of LLM reliability.

</details>


### [206] [DeepLens Diagnosis Agent: Agentic Workflow Design Lets a Small Reasoning Model Compete with Frontier LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22555)
*Mahmood Bayeshi,Veysel Kocaman,Muhammed Ali Naqvi,Yigit Gul,David Talby*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DeepLens诊断智能体，通过五阶段流水线流程（结构化临床提取、检索增强生成、约束候选生成、证据三角验证、可审计决策）结合小型医学推理模型（JSL Medical Small 7B v2），在DiagnosisArena基准上实现60.14%的Top-1诊断准确率，相比无流程版本提升36个百分点，且成本低于Claude Sonnet 4.5和Gemini 3.1 Pro。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前沿大语言模型作为通用模型，在单次提示下的诊断推理能力脆弱，缺乏结构化流程约束，难以应对医疗诊断中多阶段、不确定性高的复杂推理任务。

Method: 提出五阶段流水线智能体：1）结构化临床信息提取；2）基于RAG的约束检索；3）受限候选诊断生成；4）显式证据三角验证；5）可审计最终决策。核心模型为JSL Medical Small 7B v2，结合工作流约束而非单纯依赖模型规模。

Result: 在915例DiagnosisArena上Top-1准确率60.14%，超越所有中小型模型；相比无流程版本（23.99%）提升36个百分点；成本每例0.0072美元（24K tokens），比Claude Sonnet 4.5和Gemini 3.1 Pro低35-45%，同时准确率分别高出9.70pp和9.17pp。

Conclusion: 流程约束比模型参数规模或API成本更重要；结构化流水线可纠正前沿模型失败案例；生成的中间产物支持审计、可溯源和错误定位，适用于高风险的医疗场景。

Abstract: Medical diagnosis is a multi-stage process: extract facts, consult knowledge, generate a differential analysis, and select the best diagnosis with explanations. Frontier LLMs are strong generalists, but single-shot prompting often yields brittle diagnostic reasoning. We present the DeepLens Diagnosis Agent, a five-stage harnessing pipeline (combining model capabilities with disciplined process constraints) centered on a small medical reasoning model (JSL Medical Small 7B v2) and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). The pipeline enforces structured clinical extraction, disciplined retrieval, constrained candidate generation, explicit evidence triangulation, and an auditable final decision. On the 915-case DiagnosisArena benchmark, the agent achieved 60.14% top-1 diagnostic accuracy, the highest among small and medium-sized models. The same model without the agent workflow achieved 23.99%, a +36-point gain from workflow design alone, despite 88.2% on standard medical benchmarks, showing that diagnostic reasoning under uncertainty requires more than knowledge recall. The agent costs USD 0.0072 per case (24K tokens on A100) with 24-second latency, 35-45% cheaper than Claude Sonnet 4.5 (USD 0.0110) and Gemini 3.1 Pro (USD 0.0128) while outperforming them by +9.70pp and +9.17pp. Harnessing can also correct frontier model failures; workflow constraints can outweigh parameter count or API cost.
  Beyond aggregate accuracy, the pipeline produces structured intermediate artifacts that make each stage inspectable and support error localization. These properties support high-stakes settings where traceability, reproducibility, and auditable evidence matter alongside benchmark performance.

</details>


### [207] [MIITA: Memory-Induced Inference-Time Adaptation for Continual Learning with Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22556)
*Dong Li,Yanchi Liu,Xujiang Zhao,Wei Cheng,Zhengzhang Chen,Xintao Wu,Zhong Chen,Chen Zhao,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MIITA是一种针对资源受限场景下小语言模型的持续学习方法，通过记忆驱动的推理时适配机制，在不更新主干网络的情况下实现知识复用，有效缓解灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型在持续学习中直接更新参数会导致灾难性遗忘，而现有面向大语言模型的记忆方法依赖大存储和强上下文推理能力，不适合小语言模型。

Method: MIITA将监督经验存储为紧凑的校正方向原型与语义锚点，在推理时基于语义和不确定性线索检索，通过门控临时隐藏状态适配实现非破坏性知识复用，无需主干更新、提示扩展或测试时反向传播。

Result: 在多种监督持续学习设置下，MIITA在固定记忆预算内持续提升最终性能并缓解遗忘。

Conclusion: MIITA通过将记忆与参数解耦，为小语言模型在受限存储下的持续学习提供了一种有效的推理时适配方案。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) is essential for small language models (SLMs) to adapt to evolving real-world needs in resource-constrained deployments. However, directly updating their limited parameter space causes catastrophic forgetting. While memory-based methods naturally address this by decoupling knowledge retention from parameters, existing approaches designed for large language models (LLMs) rely on abundant storage and strong in-context reasoning that SLMs lack. To address these challenges, we propose MIITA, a Memory-Induced Inference-Time Adaptation framework for supervised CL under constrained storage. MIITA stores supervised experiences as compact correction-direction prototypes with semantic anchors, and retrieves them at inference time using semantic and uncertainty-based cues. The retrieved directions are applied through gated temporary hidden-state adaptation, enabling non-destructive reuse of past supervision without backbone updates, prompt extensions, or test-time backpropagation. A local theoretical analysis links this design to first-order loss reduction, uncertainty-guided retrieval, and directional coverage for retaining old-stage knowledge. Extensive experiments across diverse supervised CL settings show that MIITA consistently improves final performance and mitigates forgetting under fixed memory budgets.

</details>


### [208] [Codifying the Judge: Scalable Evaluation via Program Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22561)
*Tzu-Heng Huang,Shengqi Qiu,Frederic Sala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为PAJAMA的程序蒸馏方法，通过将LLM的决策逻辑蒸馏为可编程评估委员会，替代传统LLM-as-a-judge范式，在降低成本和延迟的同时保持评估性能，并可在奖励模型训练中显著提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-judge方法存在高成本、显著延迟和决策不透明等局限性，这些缺陷削弱了其可扩展性和可靠性。因此，需要一种更简单、高效的替代方案。

Method: 提出PAJAMA系统：将LLM的决策逻辑蒸馏为一组程序（programmatic judges），由委员会对候选结果进行评分，聚合形成联合裁决，并引入回退机制将低置信度案例选择性升级给LLM处理。

Result: 在5个数据集和4个模型族上，程序化评估器可匹配13B规模LLM评估器的性能。作为路由信号时，PAJAMA提升了准确率和吞吐量，推动了Pareto前沿。在RewardBench上，基于程序化判断训练出的奖励模型性能优于基于专有LLM标签训练的模型，且API成本降低两个数量级。

Conclusion: 程序蒸馏是一种可行的替代方案，兼具透明性、可编辑性和低成本优势，在评估和奖励信号生成任务上均展现出卓越效果。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-judge has become the standard for automated evaluation, but it suffers from high cost, significant latency, and opaque decisions -- limitations that undermine its scalability and reliability. We address these with a simple, efficient alternative: program distillation. Instead of prompting an LLM at the evaluation time, we distill its decision logic into a committee of programs that score candidates directly. These programmatic judges offer transparency, are easily inspected or edited, and eliminate per-sample API costs. Building on this notion, we introduce PAJAMA, a system that synthesizes programs as judges, aggregates their decisions into a joint verdict, and incorporates a fallback mechanism to selectively escalate low-confidence cases to an LLM. Across five datasets and four model families, we show that programmatic judges can match the performance of a 13B-size LLM judge. When using program outputs as routing signals, PAJAMA improves both accuracy and throughput and advances the Pareto frontier. Beyond evaluation, programmatic judges produce cheap and effective reward signals: on RewardBench, a reward model distilled from programs' verdicts outperforms one trained on a proprietary LLM's labels at two orders of magnitude lower API cost.

</details>


### [209] [SF-AMS: Strategic Forgetting for Structured Memory in LLM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22562)
*Ning Yang,Siqi Li,Miaoxin Shen,Yuan Zhou,Meng Zhang,Tong Li,Haijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SF-AMS框架，通过动态效用驱动机制建模记忆重要性，替代静态检索和启发式衰减，在长上下文推理任务中显著提升多跳推理、时间推理和开放域任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理在处理长上下文依赖时，冗余和无关信息会降低多步推理能力，现有静态检索和启发式衰减方法无法有效应对。

Method: SF-AMS采用效用驱动的生存机制，根据使用冗余度和时间信号更新记忆重要性，形成层次化记忆结构；结合Composite Importance Scoring融合语义和实体级信号增强检索鲁棒性。

Result: 在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-s上超越LightMem、MemO、A-Mem等基线；Qwen2.5-7B多跳推理F1提升+9.65，GPT-4o-mini时间推理F1+6.91，开放域任务F1+6.53。

Conclusion: 将记忆重要性建模为动态效用信号对于可靠的长上下文推理至关重要，SF-AMS展现了跨骨干网络的强泛化能力。

Abstract: Managing long-context dependencies remains a primary bottleneck in LLM agents, as redundant and irrelevant information can degrade multi-step reasoning. Strategic Forgetting for Agent Memory Systems (SF-AMS) is proposed as a framework for maintaining compact high-utility memory by modeling the long-term importance of memory units. SF-AMS replaces static retrieval and heuristic decay with a utility-driven survival mechanism that updates memory importance from usage redundancy and temporal signals, inducing a hierarchical memory structure that prioritizes stable entity-consistent information while filtering noise. On top of this, Composite Importance Scoring integrates semantic and entity level signals to improve retrieval robustness. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval-s show consistent gains over strong state of the art baselines including LightMem MemO and A-Mem. The largest improvement appears in multi-hop reasoning under Qwen2.5-7B where SF-AMS achieves plus 9.65 F1 over the strongest baseline followed by temporal reasoning under GPT-4o-mini plus 6.91 F1 and open-domain tasks plus 6.53 F1 demonstrating strong cross backbone generalization. These results show that modeling memory importance as a dynamic utility signal is critical for reliable long-context reasoning.

</details>


### [210] [Synthetic Scenario Generation for Evaluation of Industry 4.0 Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22563)
*Sagar Chethan Kumar,Rohith Kanathur,Dhaval Patel,Kaoutar El Maghraoui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ScenarioGeneratorAgent，一个基于标准的工业智能体基准测试场景合成生成流程，通过优化将Smart Grid Transformer场景生成速度提升8倍，同时保持场景质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工业智能体基准测试（如AssetOpsBench）依赖人工编写场景，覆盖资产类别有限，缺乏可扩展的高质量场景生成方法。

Method: 提出ScenarioGeneratorAgent流程，包含证据驱动的资产画像构建、覆盖感知的场景预算分配、混合验证-修复循环（保证模式有效性、工具可达性、物理合理性、标准对齐和去重），以及两级缓存、并行焦点组生成等优化策略。

Result: 在智能电网变压器场景生成中，优化后端到端运行时间减少8倍（50个场景），综合质量评分74.2±1.9，与未优化基线73.8±3.0相当。

Conclusion: 基于标准的合成场景生成能高效扩展工业智能体基准测试，且不牺牲场景质量。

Abstract: Industrial agent benchmarks require realistic evaluation scenarios that integrate telemetry, failure modes, maintenance records, and domain standards. However, existing benchmarks such as AssetOpsBench rely on manually authored scenarios and cover a limited set of asset classes. We extend AssetOpsBench with a Smart Grid Transformer asset class and four IEC-grounded diagnostic tools for health-index prediction, dissolved-gas analysis, winding-temperature assessment, and load-profile assessment. We further introduce ScenarioGeneratorAgent, a pipeline for synthetic industrial-agent scenario generation. The pipeline constructs evidence-grounded asset profiles, allocates coverage-aware scenario budgets across operational domains, and generates candidates through a hybrid validation-and-repair loop that enforces schema validity, tool reachability, physical plausibility, standards alignment, and deduplication. To improve scalability, we apply two-level caching, parallel focus-group generation, thread-pool offloading, batched LLM calls, and early rejection filtering. On Smart Grid Transformer scenario generation, these optimizations reduce end-to-end runtime by $8\times$ for 50 scenarios while preserving quality, achieving a composite quality score of $74.2 \pm 1.9$ compared with $73.8 \pm 3.0$ for the unoptimized baseline. These results show that standards-grounded synthetic scenario generation can efficiently expand industrial-agent benchmarks without sacrificing scenario quality.

</details>


### [211] [Loss-Aware Feature-Map Pruning in Convolutional Neural Networks Using Multi-Armed Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22564)
*Salem Ameen,Sunil Vadera*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于多臂老虎机的损失感知特征图剪枝框架，通过将候选特征图视为臂，在有限预算内评估并移除冗余特征图，从而减少卷积神经网络的计算和存储开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卷积神经网络中常存在冗余特征图，这些冗余特征图增加了存储和推理成本，因此需要一种有效的剪枝方法来去除冗余，同时保持模型精度。

Method: 将每个候选特征图视为一个臂，每次迭代时临时屏蔽一个特征图并在采样的迷你批次上评估，观测损失变化并将其转换为安全移除奖励。在固定预算后，根据学习到的分数排序并永久移除top-k特征图及其对应的滤波器、偏置和下一层输入通道核。使用了UCB1和Thompson Sampling两种多臂老虎机算法。

Result: 在LeNet/MNIST、CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、SVHN、CUB-200-2011和Oxford Flowers 102等数据集上，UCB1和Thompson Sampling在移除特征图并减少卷积计算的同时，保持了接近未剪枝模型的精度。Friedman和Nemenyi检验显示，UCB1获得最高平均秩，Thompson Sampling次之，两者均显著优于贪心和基于幅度的剪枝方法。

Conclusion: 基于多臂老虎机的损失感知剪枝方法能有效去除冗余特征图，在显著减少计算量的同时保持模型精度，其中UCB1和Thompson Sampling表现优异且统计上与原未剪枝模型无显著差异。

Abstract: Convolutional neural networks often contain redundant feature maps that increase storage and inference cost. This paper presents a loss-aware feature-map pruning framework using multi-armed bandits. Feature-map pruning is structured because it removes complete convolutional output channels and their producing filters rather than isolated scalar weights. Each candidate feature map is treated as an arm. At each play time, one map is temporarily masked and evaluated on a sampled mini-batch; the map is then restored and the observed loss change is converted into a safe-removal reward. After a fixed play budget, candidate maps are ranked by learned scores and the top-k maps are permanently removed with their filters, biases and corresponding next-layer input-channel kernels. The study evaluates UCB1 and Thompson Sampling, compares them with direct/oracle-style evaluation on LeNet/MNIST, and extends the evaluation to MNIST, CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, SVHN, CUB-200-2011 and Oxford Flowers 102. Results show that UCB1 and Thompson Sampling preserve accuracy close to unpruned models while removing feature maps and reducing convolutional computation. Friedman and Nemenyi tests show that UCB1 obtains the highest mean rank, followed by Thompson Sampling; both significantly outperform greedy and magnitude-based pruning while remaining statistically comparable to the original unpruned model.

</details>


### [212] [DSTFView: Multi-View Cloud-Edge Workload Forecasting with Dual-Input Spatio-Temporal-Frequency Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22565)
*Qingzhong Li,Hui Ma,Yajun Zhang,Qingchang Ma,Zhou Long*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DSTFView，一种面向云边协同环境的双输入时空频多视图工作负载预测框架，通过联合建模邻近与周期依赖、提取时空频域特征及自适应融合机制，在CPU和TP数据集上优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在云边协同环境中难以兼顾多维特征建模与预测效率，无法很好支持边缘端对延迟敏感、高并发和高可靠性的应用需求。

Method: 提出DSTFView框架，采用双输入结构联合建模邻近依赖与周期依赖，从空间、时间和频域三个维度提取特征，并设计自适应融合机制动态调整各视图贡献以捕捉突变。

Result: 在CPU和TP数据集上，DSTFView在多个预测范围与评估指标上均优于代表性基线方法。

Conclusion: DSTFView能有效平衡云边协同环境下的多维特征建模与预测效率，显著提升工作负载预测性能。

Abstract: With the widespread deployment of edge-side AI inference, edge platforms are increasingly required to support latency-sensitive, highly concurrent, and reliability-critical applications. However, existing methods often struggle to balance multidimensional feature modeling and forecasting efficiency in collaborative cloud-edge environments. To address this issue, we propose DSTFView, a dual-input spatio-temporal-frequency multi-view workload forecasting framework for collaborative cloud-edge environments. It jointly models closeness and period dependencies and extracts spatial, temporal, and frequency-domain dependencies. Besides, it designs an adaptive fusion mechanism and adjusts the contribution of each view to capture abrupt changes. Experimental results on the CPU and TP datasets demonstrate that DSTFView consistently outperforms representative baselines across multiple forecasting horizons and evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [213] [MedLoCoMo: A Long-Context Multi-Session Medical Dialogue Benchmark for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22566)
*Zeyu Zhang,Ziqing Wang,Kaize Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedLoCoMo是一个针对多入院医疗对话的医学长上下文记忆基准测试，包含100个患者时间线（平均1669.8轮对话），用于评估LLM在跨入院推理、单入院推理和对抗性不可回答问题上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学QA基准主要测试短上下文知识或单文档理解，缺乏评估LLM在纵向患者病史中运用、连接和拒绝回答能力的基准。

Method: 从MIMIC-IV和MIMIC-IV-Note构建入院级临床包，合成医患对话，生成基于证据的QA项（单入院、跨入院、对抗性不可回答三种设置），共100条患者时间线。

Result: 跨入院推理一致性地比局部证据使用更难，即使模型拥有长上下文窗口或使用外部记忆/检索方法也是如此。

Conclusion: MedLoCoMo揭示了当前LLM在纵向患者推理上的局限性，并提供了可复现的基准。

Abstract: MedLoCoMo is a Medical Long-Context Memory benchmark for patient-specific clinical reasoning over multi-admission medical dialogue. Existing medical QA benchmarks largely test short context knowledge or single document grounding, leaving open whether LLMs can use, connect, and abstain over longitudinal patient histories. We build MedLoCoMo from deidentified MIMIC-IV and MIMIC-IV-Note records by constructing admission-level clinical packets, synthesizing grounded doctor-patient conversations, and generating evidence linked QA items over single-admission, cross-admission, and adversarial unanswerable settings. The benchmark contains 100 patient timelines averaging 1,669.8 turns, 29.7 sessions, and 74,512.2 tokens per conversation. Across the evaluated baselines, cross-admission reasoning is consistently harder than localized evidence use, even when models have long context windows or use external memory or retrieval methods. The code and MedLoCoMo benchmark release is available at https://github.com/leozzy13/MedLoCoMo for use and reproducibility.

</details>


### [214] [Keyword Matters: Unveiling the Energy Sensitivity of On-Device LLM Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22568)
*Ruiyi Tao,Xiaolong Tu,Haoxin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了提示词设计对端侧大语言模型能耗的影响，通过在智能手机上进行真实功耗测量，发现提示词中的祈使动词和指令结构会影响解码长度和总能耗，表明提示工程可作为提升能效的轻量级手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然模型压缩和运行时加速已被广泛研究，但提示词设计对能耗的影响仍未被充分探索。随着LLM在电池供电的移动端和嵌入式设备上部署越来越多，高能耗成为根本性约束，因此需要探索提示工程这一轻量级手段来提升能效。

Method: 在智能手机上通过真实功耗测量，量化分析提示词的词汇特征（特别是祈使关键词）和指令结构对解码长度及总能耗的影响。

Result: 不同动词和任务之间存在一致的能耗差异，提示词设计能显著影响解码长度和总能耗。

Conclusion: 提示工程可作为提升端侧LLM能效的轻量级手段，提示词用词和结构是影响能耗的重要因素。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed on mobile and embedded devices to improve privacy and reduce network latency. Yet on-device inference faces a fundamental constraint: high energy consumption on battery-powered, resource-limited hardware. While model compression and runtime acceleration have been widely studied, the effect of \emph{prompt design} on energy efficiency remains underexplored. This paper presents an empirical study of the relationship between prompt wording and energy consumption for on-device LLMs. Using real power measurements collected on a smartphone, we quantify how linguistic features, particularly imperative keywords and instruction structure, affect decoding length and total energy. Our results show consistent energy differences across verbs and tasks, indicating that prompt engineering is a lightweight lever for improving energy efficiency.

</details>


### [215] [Execution-Grounded Security Testing for Coding Agents in Software Engineering Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22569)
*Yifei Ge,Weisong Sun,Jinkun Xiao,Yuchen Chen,Yebo Feng,Peizhuo Lv,Xia Feng,Chunrong Fang,Zhihong Zhao,Zhenyu Chen,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于执行证据的红队测试框架，通过将危险操作伪装成常规软件工程任务，成功诱导编码代理执行不安全操作，实验显示在代码载体上成功率达73.61%，文本载体上达53.93%，揭示当前系统运维中的编码代理在任务伪装下存在严重安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码代理在系统运维中的工具使用可能直接修改系统构件、执行环境和底层系统，且这些修改可能持久存在并被后续触发滥用权限，因此需要从系统层面测试其安全边界。

Method: 提出执行层面的红队测试框架，将危险操作嵌入常规软件工程任务（单元测试、回归测试、崩溃复现、验证等），利用执行预言机引导细化，通过工具调用、运行时轨迹和文件系统差异等可观察沙箱证据探测执行层安全边界。

Result: 在多代理框架和模型骨干上，红队任务重构显著增加了验证不安全执行，代码载体达73.61%，文本载体达53.93%。

Conclusion: 编码代理在系统运维中仍不安全，一旦风险意图隐藏在看似合理的工程任务中，代理会被诱导执行对周围系统的不安全操作，需要更强的安全测试和防护措施。

Abstract: Coding agents are increasingly integrated into system operations, where their tool use can directly modify project artifacts, execution environments, and the underlying system. For example, if a coding agent inserts a hook into a system startup or configuration script, that change can persist after the interaction, be triggered later, and abuse delegated user or system privileges to modify the system. This makes security testing a system problem: the key question is not only what the agent says, but what it actually does to the surrounding environment. We present an execution-grounded red-team testing framework for probing this execution-layer security boundary using observable sandbox evidence, including tool invocations, runtime traces, and file-system diffs. Our framework embeds target unsafe operations into routine software engineering workloads, including unit testing, regression testing, crash reproduction, and validation, and uses an execution oracle to guide refinement when an initial probe is rejected or fails. Across multiple agent frameworks and model backbones, our red-team workload reformulation substantially increases verified unsafe execution, reaching 73.61% on code carriers and 53.93% on text carriers.
